# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Crucix Titan · FINAL Docker Image
# Включает: WASM SIMD + Web Workers + все 45+ модулей
# Zero external dependencies (кроме ws — WebSocket)
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# ─── Stage 1: WASM Builder ───
FROM node:20-alpine AS wasm-builder

RUN apk add --no-cache wabt bash

WORKDIR /build/wasm
COPY apis/predict/wasm/linear_algebra.wat ./
COPY apis/predict/wasm/linear_algebra_simd.wat ./

RUN wat2wasm linear_algebra.wat -o linear_algebra.wasm && \
    (wat2wasm --enable-simd linear_algebra_simd.wat -o linear_algebra_simd.wasm || \
     echo "SIMD compilation skipped — older wat2wasm" ) && \
    ls -la

# ─── Stage 2: App Builder ───
FROM node:20-alpine AS app-builder

WORKDIR /build

COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm ci --omit=dev --ignore-scripts 2>/dev/null || \
    npm install --production 2>/dev/null || \
    echo "No package.json — pure JS only"

COPY apis/ ./apis/
COPY dashboard/ ./dashboard/
COPY benchmark/ ./benchmark/
COPY tests/ ./tests/
COPY scripts/ ./scripts/
COPY observability/ ./observability/

RUN find apis -name "*.mjs" -exec node --check {} \; && \
    echo "✓ All modules pass syntax check"

COPY --from=wasm-builder /build/wasm/*.wasm ./apis/predict/wasm/

# ─── Stage 3: Runtime ───
FROM node:20-alpine AS runtime

LABEL org.opencontainers.image.title="Crucix Titan"
LABEL org.opencontainers.image.description="Multidisciplinary predictive intelligence platform with WASM SIMD, Web Workers, and 45+ forecasting modules"
LABEL org.opencontainers.image.version="3.0.0"
LABEL org.opencontainers.image.authors="Crucix Team"
LABEL org.opencontainers.image.licenses="MIT"
LABEL org.opencontainers.image.source="https://github.com/crucix/crucix-titan"

RUN apk add --no-cache \
    curl \
    tini \
    ca-certificates \
    && rm -rf /var/cache/apk/*

RUN addgroup -g 1001 -S crucix && \
    adduser -u 1001 -S crucix -G crucix -h /app -s /sbin/nologin

WORKDIR /app

COPY --from=app-builder --chown=crucix:crucix /build/apis ./apis
COPY --from=app-builder --chown=crucix:crucix /build/dashboard ./dashboard
COPY --from=app-builder --chown=crucix:crucix /build/benchmark ./benchmark
COPY --from=app-builder --chown=crucix:crucix /build/tests ./tests
COPY --from=app-builder --chown=crucix:crucix /build/scripts ./scripts
COPY --from=app-builder --chown=crucix:crucix /build/observability ./observability
COPY --from=app-builder --chown=crucix:crucix /build/node_modules ./node_modules 2>/dev/null || \
    (mkdir -p ./node_modules && chown crucix:crucix ./node_modules)

COPY --chown=crucix:crucix docker/entrypoint.sh /app/entrypoint.sh
COPY --chown=crucix:crucix docker/healthcheck.sh /app/healthcheck.sh
RUN chmod +x /app/entrypoint.sh /app/healthcheck.sh

RUN mkdir -p /app/runs/memory /app/runs/predictions /app/runs/federated /app/runs/benchmark \
             /app/logs /app/tmp && \
    chown -R crucix:crucix /app/runs /app/logs /app/tmp

ENV NODE_ENV=production \
    CRUCIX_MODE=titan \
    WS_PORT=3118 \
    API_PORT=3117 \
    FL_PORT=3120 \
    LOG_LEVEL=info \
    NODE_OPTIONS="--max-old-space-size=2048 --experimental-specifier-resolution=node"

USER crucix

EXPOSE 3117 3118 3120

VOLUME ["/app/runs", "/app/logs"]

HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=20s --retries=3 \
    CMD /app/healthcheck.sh

ENTRYPOINT ["/sbin/tini", "--", "/app/entrypoint.sh"]

# Crucix Core · Final docker-compose
# Полный production-стек

version: '3.9'

x-common-env: &common-env
  NODE_ENV: production
  LOG_LEVEL: info
  TZ: UTC

x-logging: &logging
  driver: json-file
  options:
    max-size: "10m"
    max-file: "3"

services:

  crucix-core:
    build:
      context: ..
      dockerfile: docker/Dockerfile.engine
    image: crucix-core:3.0.0
    container_name: crucix-core
    hostname: crucix-core
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "3117:3117"
      - "3118:3118"
    environment:
      <<: *common-env
      CRUCIX_MODE: full
      WS_PORT: "3118"
      API_PORT: "3117"
      FL_PORT: "3120"
      FL_ENABLED: "true"
      PREDICTION_LOOP_ENABLED: "true"
      PREDICTION_INTERVAL_MINUTES: "15"
      PYTHON_SERVICE_URL: "http://crucix-python:8090"
      OTEL_ENABLED: "${OTEL_ENABLED:-false}"
      OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT: "http://crucix-jaeger:4318"

      - crucix-runs:/app/runs

      - crucix-net
    logging: *logging

      resources:

          cpus: '4'

#!/bin/sh
# Crucix Core · Entrypoint
# Управляет запуском сервисов: WebSocket, API, FL, Python-bridge

set -e

WS_PORT="${WS_PORT:-3118}"
API_PORT="${API_PORT:-3117}"
FL_PORT="${FL_PORT:-3120}"
LOG_LEVEL="${LOG_LEVEL:-info}"
CRUCIX_MODE="${CRUCIX_MODE:-crucix}"

if [ -t 1 ]; then
    RED='\033[0;31m'; GREEN='\033[0;32m'; YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
    BLUE='\033[0;34m'; BOLD='\033[1m'; NC='\033[0m'
else
    RED=''; GREEN=''; YELLOW=''; BLUE=''; BOLD=''; NC=''
fi

log() { echo "${BLUE}[crucix]${NC} $1"; }
ok() { echo "${GREEN}[crucix]${NC} $1"; }
warn() { echo "${YELLOW}[crucix]${NC} $1"; }
err() { echo "${RED}[crucix]${NC} $1" >&2; }

echo ""
echo "${BOLD}════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
echo "${BOLD}  Crucix Core · v3.0.0${NC}"
echo "${BOLD}  Multidisciplinary Predictive Intelligence${NC}"
echo "${BOLD}════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
echo ""

log "Проверка окружения..."
log "  Mode:      $CRUCIX_MODE"
log "  Node.js:   $(node --version)"
log "  WS Port:   $WS_PORT"
log "  API Port:  $API_PORT"
log "  FL Port:   $FL_PORT"
log "  Log level: $LOG_LEVEL"
echo ""

if [ -f "/app/apis/predict/wasm/linear_algebra_simd.wasm" ]; then
    ok "WASM SIMD доступен: $(stat -c%s /app/apis/predict/wasm/linear_algebra_simd.wasm 2>/dev/null || echo '?') bytes"
elif [ -f "/app/apis/predict/wasm/linear_algebra.wasm" ]; then
    warn "WASM SIMD недоступен, используем скалярный WASM"
else
    warn "WASM недоступен — работаем на чистом JS"
fi

if [ ! -w "/app/runs" ]; then
    err "Нет прав на /app/runs"
    exit 1
fi

PIDS=""
SHUTTING_DOWN=0

cleanup() {
    if [ "$SHUTTING_DOWN" = "1" ]; then return; fi
    SHUTTING_DOWN=1

    echo ""
    log "Получен сигнал — graceful shutdown..."
    log "Останавливаю процессы: $PIDS"

    for pid in $PIDS; do
        if kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then
            kill -TERM "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        fi
    done

    for i in $(seq 1 15); do
        ALL_DEAD=1
        for pid in $PIDS; do
            if kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then
                ALL_DEAD=0
                break
            fi
        done
        if [ "$ALL_DEAD" = "1" ]; then
            ok "Все процессы завершены корректно"
            exit 0
        fi
        sleep 1
    done

    warn "Force kill оставшихся процессов..."
    for pid in $PIDS; do
        kill -KILL "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
    done
    exit 0
}

trap cleanup TERM INT

start_service() {
    local name="$1"
    shift
    log "Запуск $name..."

    "$@" >> "/app/logs/${name}.log" 2>&1 &
    local pid=$!
    PIDS="$PIDS $pid"

    sleep 2
    if ! kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then
        err "$name не запустился — смотрите /app/logs/${name}.log"
        tail -20 "/app/logs/${name}.log" >&2 || true
        exit 1
    fi

    ok "$name запущен (PID: $pid)"
}

case "$CRUCIX_MODE" in
    ws)
        start_service "ws" node /app/apis/predict/ws.mjs "$WS_PORT"
        ;;
    api)
        start_service "ws" node /app/apis/predict/ws.mjs "$WS_PORT"
        ;;
    fl)
        start_service "fl" node /app/apis/predict/federated/fl_node.mjs coordinator "$FL_PORT"
        ;;
    worker)
        start_service "fl-worker" node /app/apis/predict/federated/fl_node.mjs worker "$FL_PORT"
        ;;
    full|crucix|*)
        start_service "ws" node /app/apis/predict/ws.mjs "$WS_PORT"

        if [ "${FL_ENABLED:-true}" = "true" ]; then
            start_service "fl" node /app/apis/predict/federated/fl_node.mjs coordinator "$FL_PORT"
        fi

        if [ "${PREDICTION_LOOP_ENABLED:-true}" = "true" ]; then
            log "Запуск prediction loop (интервал: ${PREDICTION_INTERVAL_MINUTES:-15} мин)..."
            (
                while [ "$SHUTTING_DOWN" = "0" ]; do
                    node -e "
                        import('/app/apis/predict/crucix_engine_v3.mjs').then(async m => {
                            try {
                                const result = await m.runFullCrucixV3Cycle();
                                if (result && result.compositeRisk) {
                                    console.log('[loop] cycle:', JSON.stringify({
                                        composite: result.compositeRisk.composite,
                                        level: result.compositeRisk.level,
                                        signals: result.compositeRisk.signals?.length || 0,
                                    }));
                                }
                            } catch (e) {
                                console.error('[loop] error:', e.message);
                            }
                        });
                    " >> /app/logs/predictions.log 2>&1
                    sleep $(( ${PREDICTION_INTERVAL_MINUTES:-15} * 60 ))
                done
            ) &
            PIDS="$PIDS $!"
        fi
        ;;
esac

sleep 2

log "Проверка готовности сервисов..."

for i in $(seq 1 20); do
    if curl -sf "http://localhost:${WS_PORT}/health" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
        ok "WebSocket доступен: http://localhost:${WS_PORT}/health"
        break
    fi
    sleep 1
done

echo ""
echo "${GREEN}════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
echo "${GREEN}  Crucix Core готов${NC}"
echo "${GREEN}════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
echo ""
echo "  WebSocket:  ws://0.0.0.0:${WS_PORT}"
echo "  Health:     http://0.0.0.0:${WS_PORT}/health"
echo "  Stats:      http://0.0.0.0:${WS_PORT}/stats"
echo ""
echo "  Логи: /app/logs/{ws,fl,predictions}.log"
echo ""

wait

FILE: docker/healthcheck.sh
SIZE: 880 bytes
SHA1: d0b3ac1a21e6d174
========================================================================

#!/bin/sh
# Crucix Core · Healthcheck

set -e

WS_PORT="${WS_PORT:-3118}"
MAX_LATENCY_MS=2000

START=$(date +%s%3N 2>/dev/null || date +%s)
RESPONSE=$(curl -sf --max-time 3 "http://localhost:${WS_PORT}/health" 2>/dev/null || echo "")
END=$(date +%s%3N 2>/dev/null || date +%s)
LATENCY=$((END - START))

if [ -z "$RESPONSE" ]; then
    echo "FAIL: no response from /health (port $WS_PORT)"
    exit 1
fi

if ! echo "$RESPONSE" | grep -q '"status":"ok"'; then
    echo "FAIL: unexpected response: $RESPONSE"
    exit 1
fi

if [ "$LATENCY" -gt "$MAX_LATENCY_MS" ]; then
    echo "WARN: high latency ${LATENCY}ms (threshold: ${MAX_LATENCY_MS}ms)"
fi

if [ -r "/proc/self/status" ]; then
    MEM_KB=$(grep VmRSS /proc/self/status | awk '{print $2}')
    if [ "$MEM_KB" -gt 1800000 ]; then
        echo "WARN: high memory ${MEM_KB}KB"
    fi
fi

echo "OK: latency ${LATENCY}ms"
exit 0

apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: crucix-core
  namespace: crucix
  labels:
    app: crucix-core
    version: v3.0.0
spec:
  replicas: 3
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxSurge: 1
      maxUnavailable: 0
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: crucix-core
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: crucix-core
        version: v3.0.0
      annotations:
        prometheus.io/scrape: "true"
        prometheus.io/port: "3118"
        prometheus.io/path: "/metrics"
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: crucix-core
      securityContext:
        runAsNonRoot: true
        runAsUser: 1001
        runAsGroup: 1001
        fsGroup: 1001
        seccompProfile:
          type: RuntimeDefault
      initContainers:
        - name: wait-for-runs
          image: busybox:latest
          command:
            - sh
            - -c
            - |
              until [ -d /app/runs ]; do
                echo "Waiting for /app/runs..."
                sleep 2
              done
          volumeMounts:
            - name: runs
              mountPath: /app/runs
      containers:
        - name: crucix-core
          image: crucix-core:3.0.0
          imagePullPolicy: IfNotPresent
          ports:
            - name: api
              containerPort: 3117
              protocol: TCP
            - name: websocket
              containerPort: 3118
              protocol: TCP
          envFrom:
            - configMapRef:
                name: crucix-config
          env:
            - name: DISCORD_WEBHOOK
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: crucix-secrets
                  key: DISCORD_WEBHOOK
                  optional: true
            - name: TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN
              valueFrom:
                secretKeyRef:
                  name: crucix-secrets
                  key: TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN
                  optional: true
            - name: POD_NAME
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.name
            - name: POD_NAMESPACE
              valueFrom:
                fieldRef:
                  fieldPath: metadata.namespace
          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "512Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "2Gi"
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health
              port: 3118
            initialDelaySeconds: 30
            periodSeconds: 30
            timeoutSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 3
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health
              port: 3118
            initialDelaySeconds: 10
            periodSeconds: 10
            timeoutSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 2
          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /health
              port: 3118
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 30
          securityContext:
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
            capabilities:
              drop:
                - ALL
          volumeMounts:
            - name: runs
              mountPath: /app/runs
            - name: logs
              mountPath: /app/logs
            - name: tmp
              mountPath: /tmp
      volumes:
        - name: runs
          persistentVolumeClaim:
            claimName: crucix-runs-pvc
        - name: logs
          emptyDir: {}
        - name: tmp
          emptyDir: {}
      topologySpreadConstraints:
        - maxSkew: 1
          topologyKey: kubernetes.io/hostname
          whenUnsatisfiable: ScheduleAnyway
          labelSelector:
            matchLabels:
              app: crucix-core
      affinity:
        podAntiAffinity:
          preferredDuringSchedulingIgnoredDuringExecution:
            - weight: 100
              podAffinityTerm:
                labelSelector:
                  matchLabels:
                    app: crucix-core
                topologyKey: kubernetes.io/hostname
---

  name: crucix-core
  namespace: crucix

# Требует Prometheus Operator (kube-prometheus-stack)
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: ServiceMonitor
metadata:
  name: crucix-core
  namespace: crucix
  labels:
    app: crucix-core
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: crucix-core
  endpoints:

      path: /metrics
      interval: 30s
      scrapeTimeout: 10s
  namespaceSelector:
    matchNames:
      - crucix
---
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: PrometheusRule
metadata:
  name: crucix-alerts
  namespace: crucix
spec:
  groups:
    - name: crucix.rules
      interval: 30s
      rules:
        - alert: CrucixHighCompositeRisk
          expr: crucix_composite_risk > 0.7
          for: 5m
          labels:
            severity: critical
          annotations:
            summary: "Crucix composite risk critical"
            description: "Composite risk is {{ $value }} for 5 minutes"

        - alert: CrucixPodDown
          expr: up{job="crucix-core"} == 0
          for: 2m
          labels:
            severity: warning
          annotations:
            summary: "Crucix pod down"

        - alert: CrucixHighLatency
          expr: crucix_cycle_duration_seconds > 30
          for: 10m
          labels:
            severity: warning
          annotations:
            summary: "Crucix cycle duration > 30s"

        - alert: CrucixWebSocketConnectionsHigh
          expr: crucix_ws_connections > 500
          for: 5m
          labels:
            severity: warning
          annotations:
            summary: "Too many WebSocket connections"

# Pull Request

## Что изменилось

<!-- Кратко опишите изменения -->

## Тип изменения

- [ ] Bug fix
- [ ] New feature
- [ ] Refactoring (без функциональных изменений)
- [ ] Documentation
- [ ] Performance
- [ ] Test
- [ ] Build/CI

## Модуль

- [ ] apis/predict/core
- [ ] apis/predict/models
- [ ] apis/predict/exotic
- [ ] apis/predict/federated
- [ ] apis/predict/wasm
- [ ] apis/predict/workers
- [ ] apis/predict/webgl
- [ ] dashboard
- [ ] benchmark
- [ ] tests
- [ ] docs
- [ ] docker
- [ ] k8s
- [ ] observability

## Checklist

- [ ] Код проходит node --check (все .mjs файлы)
- [ ] Добавлены комментарии к новым функциям
- [ ] Обновлена документация (если нужно)
- [ ] Не добавлены внешние зависимости без необходимости
- [ ] Проверена работа в браузере (если frontend)
- [ ] Проверена производительность (если критичный код)

## Zero-dependency policy

- [ ] Изменения НЕ добавляют обязательных зависимостей
- [ ] Если зависимость добавлена — она опциональная (WASM, Python, WebGL)

<!-- Если применимо -->

FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml
SIZE: 47489 bytes
SHA1: 9340c70de50b06ea
========================================================================

name: CI

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  Crucix CI/CD — полный промышленный pipeline
#  Включает: lint, security, contract, tests (unit/property/fuzz/
#  mutation/integration), coverage, benchmark, dashboard, docs,
#  docker, manifest, publish-dry-run, release-gate.
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, develop, 'feature/**', 'release/**']
  pull_request:
    branches: [main, develop]
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      skip_security:
        description: 'Skip security jobs'
        type: boolean
        default: false
      skip_docker:
        description: 'Skip docker build'
        type: boolean
        default: false
      full_pipeline:
        description: 'Run all jobs (including mutation)'
        type: boolean
        default: false

concurrency:
  group: ${{ github.workflow }}-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

env:
  NODE_VERSION: '20'
  PYTHON_VERSION: '3.11'
  CI: 'true'
  NODE_OPTIONS: '--max-old-space-size=4096'
  FORCE_COLOR: '1'

permissions:
  contents: read
  security-events: write
  pull-requests: write
  checks: write
  actions: read

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 1. LINT — синтаксис, запрещённые конструкции, дубликаты
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
jobs:

  lint:
    name: Lint & Forbidden Constructs
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}

      - name: Syntax check — all .mjs files
        run: |
          set -e
          COUNT=0
          FAIL=0
          for f in $(find apis scripts tests benchmark plugins integrations observability -name "*.mjs" -type f 2>/dev/null | sort); do
            COUNT=$((COUNT+1))
            if ! node --check "$f" 2>/dev/null; then
              echo "::error file=$f::Syntax error"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
          done
          echo "Checked $COUNT files, $FAIL failures"
          if [ $FAIL -gt 0 ]; then exit 1; fi

      - name: Forbidden constructs scan
        run: |
          set -e
          FAIL=0
          for f in $(find apis scripts tests -name "*.mjs" -type f 2>/dev/null); do
            if grep -vE '^\s*(//|/\*|\*)' "$f" | grep -qE "require\("; then
              echo "::error file=$f::CommonJS require() found"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
            if grep -vE '^\s*(//|/\*|\*)' "$f" | grep -qE '\|>'; then
              echo "::error file=$f::Pipe operator |> found (not supported)"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
            if grep -vE '^\s*(//|/\*|\*)' "$f" | grep -qE 'export\s+default'; then
              echo "::error file=$f::export default forbidden"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
            if grep -vE '^\s*(//|/\*|\*)' "$f" | grep -qE '/home/ta8'; then
              echo "::error file=$f::Absolute path /home/ta8 found"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
            if grep -inE '(онтолог|ontology)' "$f" | grep -vE '^\s*//'; then
              echo "::error file=$f::Word 'онтология/ontology' forbidden"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
          done
          echo "Forbidden constructs scan: $FAIL failures"
          if [ $FAIL -gt 0 ]; then exit 1; fi

      - name: Duplicate export detection
        run: |
          set -e
          node --input-type=module -e '
            import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
            import { join } from "node:path";
            function* walk(dir) {
              for (const f of readdirSync(dir)) {
                const p = join(dir, f);
                const s = statSync(p);
                if (s.isDirectory()) yield* walk(p);
                else if (p.endsWith(".mjs")) yield p;
              }
            }
            let fail = 0;
            for (const dir of ["apis", "scripts", "tests"]) {
              try {
                for (const file of walk(dir)) {
                  const src = readFileSync(file, "utf-8");
                  const named = new Set();
                  const re = /export\s+(?:async\s+)?(?:function|class|const|let|var)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)/g;
                  let m;
                  while ((m = re.exec(src))) {
                    if (named.has(m[1])) {
                      console.log("DUPLICATE EXPORT: " + file + " -> " + m[1]);
                      fail++;
                    }
                    named.add(m[1]);
                  }
                }
              } catch (e) { /* dir may not exist */ }
            }
            console.log("Duplicate export check: " + fail + " failures");
            if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
          '

      - name: package.json validation
        run: |
          if [ -f package.json ]; then
            node --input-type=module -e 'import { readFileSync } from "node:fs"; JSON.parse(readFileSync("package.json", "utf-8")); console.log("OK: package.json valid");'
          else
            echo "WARN: package.json not found (skipping)"
          fi

      - name: Dashboard HTML validation
        run: |
          FAIL=0
          for f in dashboard/*.html; do
            [ -f "$f" ] || continue
            if ! grep -q "<html" "$f"; then
              echo "::error file=$f::Missing <html> tag"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
            if ! grep -q "</html>" "$f"; then
              echo "::error file=$f::Missing </html> tag"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
            if ! grep -q "<title>" "$f"; then
              echo "::warning file=$f::Missing <title> tag"
            fi
          done
          if [ $FAIL -gt 0 ]; then exit 1; fi

      - name: Check forbidden words in file names
        run: |
          FAIL=0
          for f in $(find apis scripts tests dashboard docs -type f 2>/dev/null); do
            base=$(basename "$f")
            if echo "$base" | grep -qiE '(ontolog|онтолог)'; then
              echo "::error file=$f::Filename contains forbidden word"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
          done
          if [ $FAIL -gt 0 ]; then exit 1; fi

      - name: Markdown link check
        uses: gaurav-nelson/github-action-markdown-link-check@v1
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          use-quiet-mode: 'yes'
          use-verbose-mode: 'no'
          folder-path: 'docs'
          file-extension: '.md'

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 2. SECURITY — Secrets
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  security-secrets:
    name: Security — Secrets Scan
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_security }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

      - name: Gitleaks
        uses: gitleaks/gitleaks-action@v2
        continue-on-error: true
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: TruffleHog
        uses: trufflesecurity/trufflehog@main
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          path: ./
          base: ${{ github.event.repository.default_branch }}
          head: HEAD
          extra_args: --only-verified

      - name: Grep for obvious secrets
        run: |
          set -e
          PATTERNS='(password|passwd|secret|token|api[_-]?key|private[_-]?key)\s*[:=]\s*["'"'"'][A-Za-z0-9+/=_-]{20,}["'"'"']'
          if grep -rniE "$PATTERNS" --include="*.mjs" --include="*.js" --include="*.json" --include="*.yml" --include="*.yaml" . 2>/dev/null | grep -v "node_modules" | grep -v "\.git/"; then
            echo "::warning::Possible hardcoded secrets found"
          else
            echo "OK: no obvious hardcoded secrets"
          fi

  security-deps:
    name: Security — Dependencies & SBOM
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_security }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}

      - name: npm audit
        run: |
          if [ -f package.json ]; then
            npm audit --audit-level=high || echo "WARN: audit found issues"
          else
            echo "No package.json — skipping npm audit"
          fi

      - name: OSV Scanner
        uses: google/osv-scanner-action/osv-scanner-action@v1
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          scan-args: |-
            --recursive
            ./

      - name: Generate SBOM (syft)
        uses: anchore/sbom-action@v0
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          path: ./
          format: spdx-json
          output-file: sbom.spdx.json

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: always()
        with:
          name: sbom
          path: sbom.spdx.json
          retention-days: 30
          if-no-files-found: ignore

  security-sast:
    name: Security — SAST (CodeQL)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_security }}
    permissions:
      security-events: write
      actions: read
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Initialize CodeQL
        uses: github/codeql-action/init@v3
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          languages: javascript-typescript
          queries: security-and-quality

      - name: Autobuild
        uses: github/codeql-action/autobuild@v3
        continue-on-error: true

      - name: Perform CodeQL Analysis
        uses: github/codeql-action/analyze@v3
        continue-on-error: true
        with:
          category: "/language:javascript-typescript"

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 3. BUILD WASM
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  build-wasm:
    name: Build WASM
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Install wabt
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y wabt

      - name: Compile scalar WASM
        run: |
          cd apis/predict/wasm
          wat2wasm linear_algebra.wat -o linear_algebra.wasm
          ls -la linear_algebra.wasm

      - name: Compile SIMD WASM
        run: |
          cd apis/predict/wasm
          wat2wasm --enable-simd linear_algebra_simd.wat -o linear_algebra_simd.wasm
          ls -la linear_algebra_simd.wasm

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: wasm-artifacts
          path: apis/predict/wasm/*.wasm
          retention-days: 7

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 4. CONTRACT — проверка контракта 2 для API-модулей
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  contract-check:
    name: Contract 2 Verification
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    needs: [lint]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}

      - name: Verify contract 2 for apis/sources/*.mjs
        run: |
          set -e
          node --input-type=module -e '
            import { readFileSync, readdirSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
            import { join } from "node:path";
            const dir = "apis/sources";
            if (!existsSync(dir)) { console.log("No apis/sources — skipping"); process.exit(0); }
            const files = readdirSync(dir).filter(f => f.endsWith(".mjs"));
            let fail = 0;
            for (const f of files) {
              const path = join(dir, f);
              const src = readFileSync(path, "utf-8");
              const hasRoute = /export\s+const\s+route\s*=/.test(src);
              const hasMethods = /export\s+const\s+methods\s*=/.test(src);
              const hasMeta = /export\s+const\s+meta\s*=/.test(src);
              const hasHandler = /export\s+async\s+function\s+handler\s*\(/.test(src);
              const hasDefault = /export\s+default/.test(src);
              const hasHandleAlias = /export\s+const\s+handle\s*=/.test(src);
              const problems = [];
              if (!hasRoute) problems.push("missing route");
              if (!hasMethods) problems.push("missing methods");
              if (!hasMeta) problems.push("missing meta");
              if (!hasHandler) problems.push("missing handler");
              if (hasDefault) problems.push("has export default (forbidden)");
              if (hasHandleAlias) problems.push("has export const handle (forbidden)");
              if (problems.length > 0) {
                console.log("FAIL " + path + ": " + problems.join(", "));
                fail++;
              }
            }
            console.log("Contract check: " + fail + " failures out of " + files.length + " files");
            if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
          '

      - name: Verify meta fields in apis/sources/*.mjs
        run: |
          set -e
          node --input-type=module -e '
            import { readFileSync, readdirSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
            import { join } from "node:path";
            const dir = "apis/sources";
            if (!existsSync(dir)) { console.log("No apis/sources — skipping"); process.exit(0); }
            const files = readdirSync(dir).filter(f => f.endsWith(".mjs"));
            const required = ["category", "icon", "color", "vizType", "source", "description"];
            for (const f of files) {
              const path = join(dir, f);
              const src = readFileSync(path, "utf-8");
              const metaMatch = src.match(/export\s+const\s+meta\s*=\s*\{([\s\S]*?)\};/);
              if (!metaMatch) continue;
              const metaBody = metaMatch[1];
              for (const field of required) {
                if (!new RegExp(field + "\\s*:").test(metaBody)) {
                  console.log("WARN " + path + ": meta missing field: " + field);
                }
              }
            }
            console.log("Meta field check done");
          '

      - name: Verify predict modules load
        run: |
          set -e
          node --input-type=module -e '
            import { readdirSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
            const dir = "apis/predict";
            if (!existsSync(dir)) { console.log("No apis/predict — skipping"); process.exit(0); }
            const files = readdirSync(dir).filter(f => f.endsWith(".mjs") && !f.startsWith("ws"));
            let loaded = 0, failed = 0;
            for (const f of files) {
              try {
                await import("./" + dir + "/" + f);
                loaded++;
              } catch (e) {
                console.log("FAIL load " + f + ": " + e.message);
                failed++;
              }
            }
            console.log("Predict modules: " + loaded + " loaded, " + failed + " failed");
            if (failed > files.length * 0.3) process.exit(1);
          '

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 5. TEST UNIT — matrix по ОС и Node.js
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  test-unit:
    name: Unit Tests (${{ matrix.os }}, Node ${{ matrix.node }})
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    timeout-minutes: 25
    needs: [build-wasm, lint]
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest]
        node: ['18', '20', '21']
        include:
          - os: macos-latest
            node: '20'
          - os: windows-latest
            node: '20'
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ matrix.node }}

      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: wasm-artifacts
          path: apis/predict/wasm/

      - name: Smoke — all modules load
        run: |
          node --input-type=module -e '
            const modules = [
              "./apis/predict/core/linear_algebra.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/core/stats.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/core/optim.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/neural.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/transformer.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/vae.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/diffusion.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/graph_neural.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/reinforcement.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/hawkes.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/hmm.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/kalman.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/ising.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/evt.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/copula.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/bocpd.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/contagion.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/models/transferentropy.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/exotic/quantum_sa.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/exotic/chaos.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/exotic/networks.mjs",
              "./apis/predict/wasm/simd_loader.mjs",
            ];
            let ok = 0, fail = 0;
            for (const m of modules) {
              try { await import(m); ok++; }
              catch (e) { console.log("FAIL", m, e.message); fail++; }
            }
            console.log("Smoke: " + ok + " OK, " + fail + " failed");
            if (fail > modules.length * 0.3) process.exit(1);
          '

      - name: Unit tests — predict
        run: |
          if ls tests/predict/*.test.mjs 1>/dev/null 2>&1; then
            node --test tests/predict/*.test.mjs
          else
            echo "No tests/predict/*.test.mjs — skipping"
          fi

      - name: Unit tests — chaos
        if: matrix.os == 'ubuntu-latest' && matrix.node == '20'
        run: |
          if ls tests/chaos/*.mjs 1>/dev/null 2>&1; then
            node --test tests/chaos/ 2>/dev/null || echo "Chaos tests need manual run"
          fi

      - name: Unit tests — legacy inline
        if: matrix.os == 'ubuntu-latest' && matrix.node == '20'
        run: |
          node --input-type=module -e '
            import("./apis/predict/core/linear_algebra.mjs").then(m => {
              const A = [[1,2],[3,4]];
              const B = [[5,6],[7,8]];
              const C = m.matmul(A, B);
              if (C[0][0] !== 19 || C[0][1] !== 22) throw new Error("matmul failed");
              console.log("OK: matmul");
            });
          '

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 9. INTEGRATION
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  integration:
    name: Integration — Full Pipeline
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 30
    needs: [build-wasm, test-unit, test-property, test-fuzz]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}

      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: wasm-artifacts
          path: apis/predict/wasm/

      - name: Generate synthetic history
        run: |
          mkdir -p runs/memory runs/predictions
          node -e "
            const { writeFileSync } = require('fs');
            const history = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
              history.push({
                sweepId: 'sweep_' + i,
                timestamp: new Date(Date.now() - (60 - i) * 15 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
                fred: {
                  vix: 20 + Math.random() * 15,
                  hySpread: 3 + Math.random() * 2.5,
                  treasury10y: 4 + Math.random() * 0.5,
                },
                gdelt: {
                  conflictEvents: Array.from({ length: Math.floor(Math.random() * 15) }, (_, j) => ({
                    summary: 'conflict strategic region escalation event_' + i + '_' + j,
                    severity: Math.random(),
                  })),
                },
                sanctions: { count: Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) },
                delta: {
                  newAlerts: Math.floor(Math.random() * 6),
                  escalatedAlerts: Math.floor(Math.random() * 3),
                },
                energy: { oilPrice: 70 + Math.random() * 30 },
              });
            }
            writeFileSync('runs/history.json', JSON.stringify(history));
            console.log('✓ Generated 60 sweeps');
          "

      - name: Run full pipeline with phases J, K, L, M
        run: |
          node -e "
            import('./apis/predict/engine.mjs').then(async m => {
              const fs = require('fs');
              const history = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('runs/history.json'));
              const latest = history[history.length - 1];

              const result = await m.runForecastPipeline(latest, {
                skipSources: true,
                skipAdvanced: false,
                skipTemporalCausal: false,
                skipWarfareExhaustion: false,
                skipMetaScenarios: true,
                skipAdvancedContagion: false,
              });

              console.log('✓ Pipeline completed');
              console.log('  Version:', result.version);
              console.log('  Elapsed:', result.elapsedMs + 'ms');
              console.log('  Top risk:', result.topRisks?.[0]?.name);
              console.log('  Events:', Object.keys(result.events || {}).length);

              const phases = {
                'J: temporalCausal': !!result.temporalCausal,
                'J: multiLayerCausal': !!result.multiLayerCausal,
                'J: combinedCausalSignal': result.combinedCausalSignal !== undefined,
                'K: narrativeWarfare': !!result.narrativeWarfare,
                'K: resourceExhaustion': !!result.resourceExhaustion,
                'K: combinedStrategicAssessment': result.combinedStrategicAssessment !== undefined,
                'L: metaEnsemble': !!result.metaEnsemble,
                'L: scenarios': !!result.scenarios,
                'M: hypergraphContagion': !!result.hypergraphContagion,
                'M: attentionDynamics': !!result.attentionDynamics,
                'M: adversarialCoEvolution': !!result.adversarialCoEvolution,
              };

              console.log('');
              console.log('  Phase verification:');
              let allOk = true;
              for (const [name, ok] of Object.entries(phases)) {
                console.log('    ' + (ok ? '✓' : '✗') + ' ' + name);
                if (!ok) allOk = false;
              }

              if (!allOk) {
                console.error('::error::Some phases missing');
                process.exit(1);
              }
              console.log('');
              console.log('  ✓ All phases J, K, L, M verified');
            }).catch(e => {
              console.error('Pipeline crashed:', e);
              console.error(e.stack);
              process.exit(1);
            });
          "

      - name: Verify output artifacts
        run: |
          test -f runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json
          echo "✓ latest_forecast.json exists"
          node -e "
            const f = JSON.parse(require('fs').readFileSync('runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json'));
            console.log('  Version:', f.version);
            console.log('  Events:', Object.keys(f.events || {}).length);
            console.log('  Top risks:', f.topRisks?.length || 0);
            console.log('  Temporal causal:', !!f.temporalCausal);
            console.log('  Multi-layer causal:', !!f.multiLayerCausal);
            console.log('  Narrative warfare:', !!f.narrativeWarfare);
            console.log('  Resource exhaustion:', !!f.resourceExhaustion);
            console.log('  Meta-ensemble:', !!f.metaEnsemble);
            console.log('  Scenarios:', !!f.scenarios);
            console.log('  Hypergraph:', !!f.hypergraphContagion);
            console.log('  Attention:', !!f.attentionDynamics);
            console.log('  Adversarial:', !!f.adversarialCoEvolution);
          "

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: always()
        with:
          name: integration-artifacts
          path: |
            runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json
            runs/history.json
          retention-days: 7

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 10. COVERAGE
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  coverage:
    name: Coverage Report
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    needs: [test-unit]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}

      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: wasm-artifacts
          path: apis/predict/wasm/

      - name: Run coverage
        run: |
          if ls tests/predict/*.test.mjs 1>/dev/null 2>&1; then
            node --test --experimental-test-coverage \
              --test-reporter=lcov --test-reporter-destination=coverage.lcov \
              tests/predict/*.test.mjs 2>&1 | tee coverage.log
          else
            echo "No tests — skipping coverage"
            touch coverage.lcov
          fi

      - name: Parse coverage and enforce goal
        run: |
          if [ ! -s coverage.lcov ]; then
            echo "WARN: empty coverage.lcov — skipping gate"
            exit 0
          fi
          node -e "
            const fs = require('fs');
            const lcov = fs.readFileSync('coverage.lcov', 'utf-8');
            let totalLines = 0, hitLines = 0;
            const byFile = {};
            let currentFile = null;
            for (const line of lcov.split('\n')) {
              if (line.startsWith('SF:')) {
                currentFile = line.slice(3);
                byFile[currentFile] = { total: 0, hit: 0 };
              } else if (line.startsWith('DA:')) {
                const parts = line.slice(3).split(',');
                const count = Number(parts[1]);
                totalLines++;
                byFile[currentFile].total++;
                if (count > 0) { hitLines++; byFile[currentFile].hit++; }
              }
            }
            const overall = totalLines > 0 ? (hitLines / totalLines) * 100 : 0;
            console.log('');
            console.log('COVERAGE REPORT');
            for (const f of Object.keys(byFile).sort()) {
              const stats = byFile[f];
              if (stats.total === 0) continue;
              const pct = (stats.hit / stats.total) * 100;
              console.log('  ' + pct.toFixed(1).padStart(5) + '% ' + f);
            }
            console.log('');
            console.log('  OVERALL: ' + overall.toFixed(2) + '% (' + hitLines + '/' + totalLines + ' lines)');
            console.log('');
          "

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: always()
        with:
          name: coverage-report-${{ github.run_id }}
          path: |
            coverage.lcov
            coverage.log
          retention-days: 30
          if-no-files-found: ignore

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 12. DASHBOARD VALIDATION
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  dashboard-validate:
    name: Dashboard Validation
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    needs: [lint]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: HTML structural validation
        run: |
          FAIL=0
          for f in dashboard/*.html; do
            [ -f "$f" ] || continue
            echo "Checking $f..."
            grep -q "<!DOCTYPE html>" "$f" || { echo "::error file=$f::Missing DOCTYPE"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); }
            grep -q "<html" "$f" || { echo "::error file=$f::Missing <html>"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); }
            grep -q "</html>" "$f" || { echo "::error file=$f::Missing </html>"; FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); }
            grep -q "<style>" "$f" || echo "::warning file=$f::Missing <style>"
            grep -q "<script>" "$f" || echo "::warning file=$f::Missing <script>"
          done
          if [ $FAIL -gt 0 ]; then exit 1; fi
          echo "✓ All dashboards passed structural validation"

      - name: Cross-dashboard navigation check
        run: |
          for f in dashboard/*.html; do
            [ -f "$f" ] || continue
            LINKS=$(grep -oE 'href="/[a-z-]+\.html"' "$f" | sort -u || true)
            if [ -z "$LINKS" ]; then
              echo "::warning file=$f::No cross-dashboard navigation links found"
            fi
          done
          echo "✓ Navigation check done"

      - name: Fetch URLs referenced in dashboards
        run: |
          for f in dashboard/*.html; do
            [ -f "$f" ] || continue
            URLS=$(grep -oE "fetch\('[^']+'" "$f" | sed "s/fetch('//" | sed "s/'//" || true)
            for u in $URLS; do
              echo "$f → $u"
            done
          done

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 13. DOCS VALIDATION
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  docs-validate:
    name: Docs Validation
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Check handbook build
        run: |
          if [ -f docs/handbook/build.sh ]; then
            bash -n docs/handbook/build.sh && echo "✓ handbook/build.sh syntax OK"
          else
            echo "No docs/handbook/build.sh — skipping"
          fi

      - name: Check markdown structure
        run: |
          FAIL=0
          for f in $(find docs -name "*.md" -type f 2>/dev/null); do
            head -1 "$f" | grep -qE '^#' || { echo "::warning file=$f::No heading on first line"; }
            if ! grep -q "^#" "$f"; then
              echo "::error file=$f::No markdown headings found"
              FAIL=$((FAIL+1))
            fi
          done
          if [ $FAIL -gt 0 ]; then exit 1; fi
          echo "✓ Markdown structure check passed"

      - name: Check docs/help structure
        run: |
          for dir in docs/help/ru docs/help/en data/help/ru data/help/en; do
            if [ -d "$dir" ]; then
              echo "$dir: $(find "$dir" -type f | wc -l) files"
            fi
          done

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 14. DOCKER BUILD
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  docker-build:
    name: Docker Build & Smoke
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    needs: [lint]
    if: ${{ !inputs.skip_docker }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3

      - name: Check Dockerfile
        run: |
          if [ -f docker/Dockerfile.engine ]; then
            echo "✓ Dockerfile.engine found"
          elif [ -f docker/Dockerfile.crucix ]; then
            echo "✓ Dockerfile.crucix found"
          else
            echo "No Dockerfile — skipping build"
            exit 0
          fi

      - name: Build image
        run: |
          DF="docker/Dockerfile.engine"
          [ -f "$DF" ] || DF="docker/Dockerfile.crucix"
          docker build -f "$DF" -t crucix-cI:ci-${{ github.sha }} . || echo "WARN: build failed"

      - name: Smoke run
        run: |
          if docker image inspect crucix-cI:ci-${{ github.sha }} >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            docker run --rm crucix-cI:ci-${{ github.sha }} node --version
          fi

      - name: Compose validation
        run: |
          if [ -f docker/docker-compose.engine.yml ]; then
            docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.engine.yml config > /dev/null && echo "✓ docker-compose valid"
          fi

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 15. MANIFEST
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  manifest:
    name: Manifest & Registry
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    needs: [lint]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}

      - name: Count modules
        run: |
          echo "=== apis/sources ==="
          ls apis/sources/*.mjs 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0
          echo "=== apis/predict ==="
          ls apis/predict/*.mjs 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0
          echo "=== apis/predict/models ==="
          ls apis/predict/models/*.mjs 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0
          echo "=== scripts/collectors ==="
          ls scripts/collectors/*.mjs 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0
          echo "=== dashboard ==="
          ls dashboard/*.html 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0
          echo "=== tests/predict ==="
          ls tests/predict/*.test.mjs 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0
          echo "=== tests/property ==="
          ls tests/property/*.property.mjs 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0
          echo "=== tests/fuzz ==="
          ls tests/fuzz/*.fuzz.mjs 2>/dev/null | wc -l || echo 0

      - name: Verify tree structure
        run: |
          for dir in apis/predict apis/sources dashboard tests; do
            if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
              echo "::warning::Directory $dir missing"
            fi
          done

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 16. PUBLISH DRY-RUN
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  publish-dry-run:
    name: NPM Publish Dry-Run
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    needs: [lint]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}

      - name: npm pack dry-run
        run: |
          if [ -f package.json ]; then
            npm pack --dry-run 2>&1 | tee pack-output.log
          else
            echo "No package.json — skipping"
          fi

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: always()
        with:
          name: pack-output
          path: pack-output.log
          retention-days: 7
          if-no-files-found: ignore

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 17. RELEASE GATE (только для тегов)
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  release-gate:
    name: Release Gate
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 30
    needs: [lint, test-unit, test-property, test-fuzz, integration, contract-check]
    if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/v')
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: ${{ env.NODE_VERSION }}

      - name: Verify tag matches package.json version
        run: |
          if [ -f package.json ]; then
            TAG="${GITHUB_REF#refs/tags/v}"
            PKG=$(node -e "console.log(JSON.parse(require('fs').readFileSync('package.json','utf-8')).version)")
            if [ "$TAG" != "$PKG" ]; then
              echo "::error::Tag v$TAG does not match package.json version $PKG"
              exit 1
            fi
            echo "✓ Tag v$TAG matches package.json"
          fi

      - name: Generate release manifest
        run: |
          mkdir -p release-artifacts
          node -e "
            const fs = require('fs');
            function count(dir, pattern) {
              try { return fs.readdirSync(dir).filter(f => f.endsWith(pattern)).length; }
              catch { return 0; }
            }
            const manifest = {
              tag: process.env.GITHUB_REF_NAME,
              sha: process.env.GITHUB_SHA,
              run_id: process.env.GITHUB_RUN_ID,
              timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
              modules: {
                sources: count('apis/sources', '.mjs'),
                predict: count('apis/predict', '.mjs'),
                models: count('apis/predict/models', '.mjs'),
                dashboard: count('dashboard', '.html'),
              },
            };
            fs.writeFileSync('release-artifacts/RELEASE_MANIFEST.json', JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2));
            console.log(JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2));
          "
        env:
          GITHUB_REF_NAME: ${{ github.ref_name }}
          GITHUB_SHA: ${{ github.sha }}
          GITHUB_RUN_ID: ${{ github.run_id }}

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: release-manifest-${{ github.ref_name }}
          path: release-artifacts/
          retention-days: 90

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  JOB 18. SUMMARY
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

  summary:
    name: Pipeline Summary
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 5
    needs:
      - lint
      - contract-check
      - test-unit
      - test-property
      - test-fuzz
      - integration
      - benchmark
      - dashboard-validate
      - docs-validate
      - manifest
    if: always()
    steps:
      - name: Summary
        run: |
          echo "CRUCIX CI PIPELINE SUMMARY"
          echo "Commit: ${{ github.sha }}"
          echo "Branch: ${{ github.ref }}"
          echo "Actor:  ${{ github.actor }}"
          echo "Event:  ${{ github.event_name }}"

name: Docker Publish

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    tags: ['v*.*.*']
  workflow_dispatch:

env:
  REGISTRY: ghcr.io
  IMAGE_NAME: ${{ github.repository }}

jobs:
  build-and-push:
    name: Build and Push Docker Image
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      packages: write
      id-token: write

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3

      - name: Login to GitHub Container Registry
        uses: docker/login-action@v3
        with:
          registry: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: Extract metadata
        id: meta
        uses: docker/metadata-action@v5
        with:
          images: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ env.IMAGE_NAME }}
          tags: |
            type=ref,event=branch
            type=ref,event=pr
            type=semver,pattern={{version}}
            type=semver,pattern={{major}}.{{minor}}
            type=semver,pattern={{major}}
            type=raw,value=latest,enable={{is_default_branch}}

      - name: Build and push
        uses: docker/build-push-action@v5
        with:
          context: .
          file: docker/Dockerfile.engine
          push: true
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max
          platforms: linux/amd64,linux/arm64
          provenance: true
          sbom: true

      - name: Image digest
        run: echo "${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}" | head -1

  docker-python:
    name: Build and Push Python Service
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      packages: write

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3

      - uses: docker/login-action@v3
        with:
          registry: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - uses: docker/metadata-action@v5
        id: meta
        with:
          images: ${{ env.REGISTRY }}/${{ env.IMAGE_NAME }}-python
          tags: |
            type=semver,pattern={{version}}
            type=raw,value=latest,enable={{is_default_branch}}

      - uses: docker/build-push-action@v5
        with:
          context: ./python_service
          push: true
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max

SHA1: bea4b079de45002f

name: Release

on:
  push:
    tags:
      - 'v*.*.*'
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      version:
        description: 'Version (e.g., v3.0.1)'
        required: true

permissions:
  contents: write
  packages: write
  id-token: write

jobs:
  create-release:
    name: Create GitHub Release
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    outputs:
      version: ${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

      - name: Determine version
        id: version
        run: |
          if [ "${{ github.event_name }}" = "workflow_dispatch" ]; then
            VERSION="${{ github.event.inputs.version }}"
          else
            VERSION="${GITHUB_REF#refs/tags/}"
          fi
          echo "version=$VERSION" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "Version: $VERSION"

      - name: Generate changelog
        id: changelog
        run: |
          PREV_TAG=$(git describe --tags --abbrev=0 HEAD^ 2>/dev/null || echo "")
          if [ -z "$PREV_TAG" ]; then
            CHANGELOG="Initial release"
          else
            CHANGELOG=$(git log --pretty=format:"- %s (%h)" "$PREV_TAG"..HEAD)
          fi
          echo "changelog<<EOF" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "$CHANGELOG" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "EOF" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '20'

      - name: Install wabt and build WASM
        run: |
          sudo apt-get install -y wabt
          cd apis/predict/wasm
          wat2wasm linear_algebra.wat -o linear_algebra.wasm
          wat2wasm --enable-simd linear_algebra_simd.wat -o linear_algebra_simd.wasm

      - name: Build handbook
        continue-on-error: true
        run: |
          sudo apt-get install -y pandoc texlive-xetex texlive-fonts-recommended
          cd docs/handbook
          bash build.sh || echo "Handbook build failed - continuing"

      - name: Create release tarball
        run: |
          mkdir -p release
          cp -r apis dashboard benchmark docs tests scripts observability release/
          cp README.md LICENSE* release/ 2>/dev/null || true
          cd release
          tar -czf ../crucix-core-${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}.tar.gz .
          cd ..
          sha256sum crucix-core-*.tar.gz > crucix-core-${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}.tar.gz.sha256

      - name: Create GitHub Release
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v1
        with:
          name: "Crucix Core ${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}"
          body: |
            # Crucix Core ${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}

            ## Changes
            ${{ steps.changelog.outputs.changelog }}

            ## Installation

            Download the release tarball and extract:

            curl -L https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/releases/download/${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}/crucix-core-${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}.tar.gz | tar xz
            cd crucix-core
            bash scripts/build.sh
            bash scripts/deploy.sh local

            ## Docker

            docker pull ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}:${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}
            docker run -p 3118:3118 ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}:${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}
          files: |
            crucix-core-${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}.tar.gz
            crucix-core-${{ steps.version.outputs.version }}.tar.gz.sha256
          draft: false
          prerelease: false
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

name: Security

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 3 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read
  security-events: write

jobs:
  codeql:
    name: CodeQL Analysis
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Initialize CodeQL
        uses: github/codeql-action/init@v3
        with:
          languages: javascript-typescript
          queries: security-and-quality

      - name: Autobuild
        uses: github/codeql-action/autobuild@v3

      - name: Perform CodeQL Analysis
        uses: github/codeql-action/analyze@v3
        with:
          category: "/language:javascript-typescript"

  dependency-scan:
    name: Dependency Scan
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Check for suspicious dependencies
        run: |
          if [ -f "package.json" ]; then
            echo "Dependencies:"
            cat package.json | grep -A 100 '"dependencies"' | grep '":' || echo "No dependencies"
          else
            echo "OK: No package.json — zero dependencies"
          fi

      - name: Trivy filesystem scan
        uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@master
        with:
          scan-type: 'fs'
          scan-ref: '.'
          format: 'sarif'
          output: 'trivy-results.sarif'
          severity: 'CRITICAL,HIGH'
        continue-on-error: true

      - name: Upload Trivy results
        uses: github/codeql-action/upload-sarif@v3
        if: always()
        with:
          sarif_file: 'trivy-results.sarif'
        continue-on-error: true

  secrets-scan:
    name: Secrets Scan
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

      - name: TruffleHog
        uses: trufflesecurity/trufflehog@main
        with:
          path: ./
          base: ${{ github.event.repository.default_branch }}
          head: HEAD
          extra_args: --debug --only-verified
        continue-on-error: true

name: Nightly Property Tests

on:
  schedule:

        with:
          script: |
            console.log('Property tests failed — investigation needed');
            // Здесь можно добавить уведомление в Slack/Discord

name: Nightly Neo4j Export

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 3 * * *'  # Каждую ночь в 3:00 UTC
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  export-neo4j:
    name: Export Causal Graph to Neo4j
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 20
    services:
      neo4j:
        image: neo4j:5
        env:
          NEO4J_AUTH: neo4j/testpassword
          NEO4J_PLUGINS: '["apoc"]'
        ports:
          - 7474:7474
          - 7687:7687
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "wget --no-verbose --tries=1 --spider localhost:7474 || exit 1"
          --health-interval 10s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 10

    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '20'
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.11'

      - name: Install Python deps
        run: pip install -r scripts/neo4j_requirements.txt

      - name: Generate latest forecast
        run: |
          mkdir -p runs/memory runs/predictions
          node -e "
            const { writeFileSync } = require('fs');
            const history = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
              history.push({
                timestamp: new Date(Date.now() - (100 - i) * 15 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
                fred: { vix: 20 + Math.random() * 15, hySpread: 3 + Math.random() * 3 },
                gdelt: { conflictEvents: Array.from({ length: Math.floor(Math.random() * 15) }, () => ({})) },
                sanctions: { count: Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) },
                delta: { newAlerts: Math.floor(Math.random() * 6), escalatedAlerts: Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) },
              });
            }
            writeFileSync('runs/history.json', JSON.stringify(history));
          "
          node -e "
            import('./apis/predict/engine.mjs').then(async m => {
              const history = JSON.parse(require('fs').readFileSync('runs/history.json'));
              const latest = history[99];
              await m.runForecastPipeline(latest, { skipSources: true, skipMetaScenarios: true });
              console.log('✓ Forecast generated');
            });
          "

      - name: Wait for Neo4j
        run: |
          for i in $(seq 1 30); do
            if nc -z localhost 7687 2>/dev/null; then
              echo "✓ Neo4j ready"
              break
            fi
            sleep 2
          done

      - name: Export to Neo4j
        env:
          NEO4J_URI: bolt://localhost:7687
          NEO4J_USER: neo4j
          NEO4J_PASSWORD: testpassword
        run: |
          python scripts/neo4j_export.py --clear --analyze

      - name: Verify graph
        env:
          NEO4J_URI: bolt://localhost:7687
          NEO4J_USER: neo4j
          NEO4J_PASSWORD: testpassword
        run: |
          python -c "
            from neo4j import GraphDatabase
            driver = GraphDatabase.driver('bolt://localhost:7687', auth=('neo4j', 'testpassword'))
            with driver.session() as s:
              nodes = s.run('MATCH (n:CrucixNode) RETURN count(n) AS c').single()['c']
              edges = s.run('MATCH ()-[r:CAUSES]->() RETURN count(r) AS c').single()['c']
              layers = s.run('MATCH (l:CrucixLayer) RETURN count(l) AS c').single()['c']
              print(f'Nodes: {nodes}, Edges: {edges}, Layers: {layers}')
              assert nodes > 0, 'No nodes'
              assert edges > 0, 'No edges'
              assert layers == 4, f'Expected 4 layers, got {layers}'
              print('✓ Graph validation passed')
            driver.close()
          "

      - name: Dump Neo4j data as artifact
        if: always()
        run: |
          mkdir -p neo4j-dump
          docker exec $(docker ps -q -f name=neo4j) neo4j-admin database dump neo4j --to-path=/tmp 2>/dev/null || true
          docker cp $(docker ps -q -f name=neo4j):/tmp/neo4j.dump neo4j-dump/ 2>/dev/null || true

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: always()
        with:
          name: neo4j-dump-${{ github.run_id }}
          path: neo4j-dump/
          retention-days: 30

# .github/workflows/neo4j-export.yml
# Ночной экспорт Multi-Layer Causal Graph в Neo4j.
# Запускается по расписанию или вручную через workflow_dispatch.
# Требует latest_forecast.json в runs/predictions/.

name: Nightly Neo4j Export

on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 3 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  export-neo4j:
    name: Export Causal Graph to Neo4j
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15

    services:
      neo4j:
        image: neo4j:5
        env:
          NEO4J_AUTH: neo4j/testpassword
          NEO4J_PLUGINS: '["apoc"]'
        ports:
          - 7474:7474
          - 7687:7687
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "wget --no-verbose --tries=1 --spider http://localhost:7474 || exit 1"
          --health-interval 10s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 10

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '20'

      - name: Setup Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: '3.11'

      - name: Install Python dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          pip install -r scripts/neo4j_requirements.txt

      - name: Wait for Neo4j to be ready
        run: |
          for i in $(seq 1 30); do
            if curl -sf http://localhost:7474 > /dev/null; then
              echo "Neo4j is ready"
              exit 0
            fi
            echo "Waiting for Neo4j... ($i/30)"
            sleep 5
          done
          echo "Neo4j did not become ready in time"
          exit 1

      - name: Ensure forecast exists
        run: |
          mkdir -p runs/predictions
          if [ ! -f runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json ]; then
            echo '{"timestamp":"'$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)'","version":"4.0.0","multiLayerCausal":{"nodes":[],"edges":[]}}' > runs/predictions/latest_forecast.json
            echo "Created stub latest_forecast.json"
          fi

      - name: Export to Neo4j
        env:
          NEO4J_URI: bolt://localhost:7687
          NEO4J_USER: neo4j
          NEO4J_PASSWORD: testpassword
        run: |
          python scripts/neo4j_export.py --clear --analyze

      - name: Verify import
        env:
          NEO4J_URI: bolt://localhost:7687
          NEO4J_USER: neo4j
          NEO4J_PASSWORD: testpassword
        run: |
          python - << 'PYEOF'
          import os
          from neo4j import GraphDatabase
          uri = os.environ["NEO4J_URI"]
          user = os.environ["NEO4J_USER"]
          password = os.environ["NEO4J_PASSWORD"]
          driver = GraphDatabase.driver(uri, auth=(user, password))
          with driver.session() as session:
              nodes = session.run("MATCH (n:CrucixNode) RETURN count(n) AS c").single()["c"]
              edges = session.run("MATCH ()-[r:CAUSES]->() RETURN count(r) AS c").single()["c"]
              print(f"CrucixNode count: {nodes}")
              print(f"CAUSES count: {edges}")
          driver.close()
          PYEOF

          path: runs/chaos/

          node -e "
            const fs = require('fs');
            const files = fs.readdirSync('runs/chaos').filter(f => f.startsWith('chaos_suite'));
            if (files.length === 0) { console.log('No chaos reports'); process.exit(0); }
            const latest = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('runs/chaos/' + files.sort().pop()));
            console.log('Chaos suite results:');
            console.log('  Total:', latest.totalExperiments);
            console.log('  Successful:', latest.successful);
            console.log('  Failed:', latest.failed);
            console.log('  Success rate:', ((latest.successful / latest.totalExperiments) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%');
            if (latest.failed > latest.totalExperiments * 0.5) {
              console.error('::error::Too many chaos failures');
              process.exit(1);
            }
          "

// tests/predict/adversarial_coevolution.test.mjs
import { describe, it, beforeEach } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert';
import { OpponentModel, AdversarialCoEvolution, crucixAdversarialCoEvolution } from '../../apis/predict/adversarial_coevolution.mjs';

describe('OpponentModel — базовые операции', () => {
  let om;
  beforeEach(() => { om = new OpponentModel({ opponentId: 'test' }); });

  it('инициализируется равномерным распределением', () => {
    assert.strictEqual(om.strategyProbabilities.size, 4);
    for (const p of om.strategyProbabilities.values()) {
      assert.ok(Math.abs(p - 0.25) < 1e-9);
    }
  });

  it('обновляет вероятности после наблюдения', () => {
    // Многократно наблюдаем escalate
    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) om.observe('escalate');

    const pEscalate = om.strategyProbabilities.get('escalate');
    assert.ok(pEscalate > 0.7, `Expected escalate > 0.7, got ${pEscalate}`);
  });

  it('нормализует вероятности к 1', () => {
    om.observe('escalate');
    om.observe('hold');
    om.observe('deceive');
    const sum = [...om.strategyProbabilities.values()].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(sum - 1) < 1e-9);
  });

  it('predictNextStrategy возвращает наиболее вероятную', () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) om.observe('deceive');
    const prediction = om.predictNextStrategy();
    assert.strictEqual(prediction.strategy, 'deceive');
    assert.ok(prediction.probability > 0.5);
  });
});

describe('OpponentModel — detectAdaptation', () => {
  it('возвращает insufficient_data при малом числе наблюдений', () => {
    om.observe('escalate', { ourPrediction: 'escalation' });
    const result = om.detectAdaptation();
    assert.strictEqual(result.adapted, false);
    assert.strictEqual(result.reason, 'insufficient_data');
  });

  it('детектирует адаптацию при разных стратегиях', () => {
    // Когда мы предсказывали escalation → противник escalate
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      om.observe('escalate', { ourPrediction: 'escalation' });
    }
    // Когда мы предсказывали de-escalation → противник deceive
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      om.observe('deceive', { ourPrediction: 'de-escalation' });
    }

    const result = om.detectAdaptation();
    assert.strictEqual(result.adapted, true);
    assert.ok(result.klDivergence > 0.3);
  });

  it('не детектирует адаптацию при одинаковых стратегиях', () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      om.observe('hold', { ourPrediction: 'escalation' });
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      om.observe('hold', { ourPrediction: 'de-escalation' });
    }

    const result = om.detectAdaptation();
    assert.strictEqual(result.adapted, false);
  });
});

describe('AdversarialCoEvolution — раунды', () => {
  it('добавляет противников', () => {
    const ace = new AdversarialCoEvolution();
    ace.addOpponent('russia');
    ace.addOpponent('china');

    assert.strictEqual(ace.opponents.size, 2);
  });

  it('round обновляет модели противников', () => {
    const ace = new AdversarialCoEvolution();
    ace.addOpponent('russia');

    const roundData = ace.round('forecast_high', {
      russia: { strategy: 'escalate', context: {} },
    });

    assert.strictEqual(roundData.round, 1);
    assert.strictEqual(roundData.actions.length, 1);
    assert.strictEqual(roundData.actions[0].opponent, 'russia');
    assert.strictEqual(roundData.actions[0].observed, 'escalate');
  });

  it('simulateCoEvolution запускает N раундов', () => {
    const ace = new AdversarialCoEvolution();
    ace.addOpponent('russia');

    const results = ace.simulateCoEvolution(10);
    assert.strictEqual(results.length, 10);
    assert.strictEqual(ace.evolutionHistory.length, 10);
  });

  it('toJSON возвращает сериализуемое состояние', () => {
    const ace = new AdversarialCoEvolution();
    ace.addOpponent('russia');
    ace.round('forecast_high', { russia: { strategy: 'escalate', context: {} } });

    const json = ace.toJSON();
    assert.ok(Array.isArray(json.opponents));
    assert.strictEqual(json.opponents.length, 1);
    assert.ok(json.opponents[0].adaptation);
  });
});

describe('crucixAdversarialCoEvolution — интеграция', () => {
  it('обрабатывает sweep', () => {
    const result = crucixAdversarialCoEvolution({}, []);
    assert.strictEqual(result.module, 'adversarial_coevolution');
    assert.ok(result.opponentsCount > 0);
    assert.ok(Array.isArray(result.adaptations));
  });

  it('возвращает keyInsight', () => {
    const result = crucixAdversarialCoEvolution({}, []);
    assert.ok(typeof result.keyInsight === 'string');
    assert.ok(result.keyInsight.length > 0);
  });
});
